MGC · 24h — the gold book: five session legs on one metal
32.44×Return on drawdown · best window
Return on drawdown · what a dollar of pain buys 32.44×

RoDD is the metric this catalog is priced on. Profit factor says the engine works; RoDD says what it costs to hold: net profit divided by the worst peak-to-valley drawdown. This system’s window: $119.5k net ÷ $3,700 max drawdown = 32.44×. Slide your drawdown budget — the projection scales with it, and so does the pain.

NOT BUILT FOR THIS SIZE The published max drawdown on this window ($3,700) exceeds a $1k budget — this sizing would not have survived the sample. Some systems are not meant to run this small.
NOT BUILT FOR THIS SIZE The published max drawdown on this window ($3,700) exceeds a $2.5k budget — this sizing would not have survived the sample. Some systems are not meant to run this small.
THIN CUSHION The published drawdown is more than half this budget — one bad stretch uses most of your room. A drawdown budget of $5k historically returned ~$162,200 over this window (≈$20,275/mo average).
RECOMMENDED SIZE The smallest budget with a full cushion over the published drawdown. A drawdown budget of $10k historically returned ~$324,400 over this window (≈$40,550/mo average).
A drawdown budget of $25k historically returned ~$811,000 over this window (≈$101,375/mo average).
A drawdown budget of $50k historically returned ~$1,622,000 over this window (≈$202,750/mo average).

Linear scaling of the published window; not a projection of future results. See the disclaimer below.
